### [1] [Spectral properties of the magnetic Robin Laplacian on curvilinear half-plane](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03783)
*Diana Barseghyan,Baruch Schneider,Yifan Zhang*

Main category: math.SP

TL;DR: 本文研究了带磁场的Robin Laplace算子在半平面区域的谱稳定性问题，证明了当磁场的紧支撑且边界做小且局部的变形时，磁Robin Laplacian的谱是稳定的，这与无磁场情形下Robin Laplacian谱对边界变形的本质不稳定性形成鲜明对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已知无磁场情形下，Robin Laplacian的谱对边界变形是不稳定的：当边界为直线时谱是纯本质谱，而边界扰动会在本质谱下方产生特征值。本文旨在探究引入磁场后是否能够改变这一不稳定性质。

Method: 考虑具有光滑边界的曲线半平面上的磁Robin Laplacian算子，磁场具有紧支集。通过分析磁Robin边界值问题的谱性质，论证磁场存在下谱在边界小且局部变形时的稳定性。

Result: 证明了具有紧支集磁场的磁Robin Laplacian在边界的小且局部的变形下，其谱是稳定的，即在本质谱下方不会因局部边界扰动而产生新的特征值。

Conclusion: 磁场（尤其具有紧支集的磁场）能够"稳定"Robin Laplacian的谱，消除无磁场情形下Robin边界条件因边界变形导致的谱不稳定现象。

Abstract: We analyse magnetic Robin Laplacian in curvilinear half-plane with a smooth boundary. It is well known that the spectrum of the Robin Laplacian is unstable with respect to boundary deformations. This means that if the boundary is a straight line then the spectrum of the Robin Laplacian is purely essential. From the other hand, the perturbation of the boundary produces eigenvalues below the essential spectrum. In this paper, the Robin-Laplace operator with a compactly supported magnetic field is considered. We prove that the spectrum of the magnetic Robin Laplacian is stable under small and local deformations of the boundary.

</details>

### [2] [Reducibility of symmetry on $q$-Painlevé equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02927)
*Chihiro Sato,Kouichi Takemura,Ohka Yamashita*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 本文研究了q-Painlevé方程与扩展仿射Weyl群表示之间的等价关系，证明了这些等价性蕴含表示的可约性，并揭示了它们与q-Painlevé方程平行平移表达式之间的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已知q-Painlevé方程具有扩展仿射Weyl群的对称性，Kajiwara、Noumi和Yamada通过引入扩展仿射Weyl群的表示建立了统一的对称性描述；而在Sakai的离散Painlevé方程理论中，平行平移对应于时间演化。本文的动机在于深入理解表示的结构及其与时间演化的关系。

Method: 本文引入了与Kajiwara、Noumi和Yamada表示之间的等价性概念，通过分析这些等价性来研究表示的可约性，并将其与q-Painlevé方程的平行平移表达式相关联。

Result: 证明了引入的等价性蕴含着表示的可约性，并阐明了这些等价性与q-Painlevé方程平行平移表达式之间的内在联系。

Conclusion: 本文建立的表示等价性不仅深化了对扩展仿射Weyl群表示结构的理解，还为q-Painlevé方程的时间演化（平行平移）提供了新的代数视角。

Abstract: It is known that $q$-Painlevé equations admit the symmetry of the extended affine Weyl groups. Kajiwara, Noumi and Yamada developed a unified description of the symmetry by introducing representations of the extended affine Weyl groups. On the other hand, a parallel translation associated with the extended affine Weyl groups describes the time evolution in Sakai's theory of discrete Painlevé equations. In this paper, we introduce the equivalences to the representations of Kajiwara, Noumi and Yamada. The equivalences imply reducibility of the representations, and they are related to the expressions of the parallel translations of the $q$-Painlevé equations.

</details>


### [3] [Certified Spherical MUSIC for 3D Localization under Adversarial Subspace Perturbations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03264)
*Albert Fannjiang,Chuong Nguyen*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 本文研究oracle子空间扰动模型下的3D稀疏定位问题，证明扰动的球形MUSIC目标函数在每个中心附近有唯一的强凸井，并给出固定步长梯度下降的收敛性分析。通过任意云框架分析得到充分条件κδ_X≳s^{2/3}，并证明该指数对绝对相干行和论证方法是最优的。确定性oracle定位模量为O(ε/κ)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MUSIC算法是经典的阵列信号/波达方向估计方法，但对其在"任意云"（arbitrary cloud）稀疏场景下，给定确定性oracle子空间扰动时的全局收敛性和定位精度缺乏严格的理论保证。

Method: 采用oracle子空间-扰动模型，将扰动后的球形MUSIC目标函数的结构特性转化为局部强凸性分析；利用任意云框架(frame analysis)和Gershgorin定理推导绝对相干行和界；通过构造上/下框架反例证明指数s^{1/6}和s^{1/3}尺度的界限，并证明s^{2/3}的充分条件最优；对可容许单点位移参数类刻画确定性定位模量。

Result: 证明每个球B_{γ/κ}(x_j)内目标函数有唯一强凸井，且井外目标值存在均匀间隙；固定步长h≍κ^{-2}的梯度下降保持井不变性并线性收敛；得到κδ_X≳s^{2/3}的充分条件，并构造反例证明该指数对绝对行和论证方法最优；最终得到确定性定位模量R(ε)≍ε/κ。

Conclusion: 本研究为任意云布设下基于扰动MUSIC的3D定位提供了严格的全局收敛与可恢复性理论保证，明确了空间分离、子空间维数与波数之间需满足的尺度关系，并通过上下界反例厘清了证明方法的内在极限。

Abstract: We study an oracle subspace-perturbation model in which the 3D localization procedure is given an \(s\)-dimensional subspace \(\widetilde{\mathcal U}\) and a deterministic error bound $\eps_{\rm sub}$ measuring the sine-theta distance between $\widetilde{\mathcal U}$ and an $s$-dimensional subspace $\cU$ of far-field patterns with wavenumber $κ$.
  Under explicit arbitrary-cloud separation and conditioning hypotheses, we prove that the perturbed spherical MUSIC objective \[
  \widetilde q(\bz)
  =
  1-\|P_{\widetilde{\mathcal U}}\varphi_\bz\|_2^2 \] has a unique strongly convex well in each ball \(B_{γ/κ}(x_j)\) and the objective has a uniform value gap outside the union of the certified wells. A fixed-step gradient map with \(h\asympκ^{-2}\) leaves every certified well invariant and converges linearly to its unique minimizer. Consequently, thresholding on an \(O(κ^{-1})\)-mesh, followed by gradient descent from all accepted grid points and duplicate removal, recovers all relevant minima.
  The arbitrary-cloud frame analysis gives the sufficient condition \[
  κδ_X\gtrsim s^{2/3} \] through an absolute coherence row sum and Gershgorin's theorem. We also construct lower-frame counterexamples below the \(s^{1/6}\) scale, upper-frame counterexamples below the \(s^{1/3}\) scale, and examples showing that the exponent \(2/3\) is optimal for the absolute-row-sum argument. The latter is a sharpness result for the proof method and is not a spectral necessity claim. Finally, for parameter classes containing a uniformly admissible one-point displacement path, we prove that the deterministic oracle localization modulus is \[
  \mathfrak R(\eps)
  \asymp
  \frac{\eps}κ. \]

</details>


### [4] [A thickness boundary and modular obstructions for two-set radial projections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03400)
*Yuuki Miwa*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 本文研究了缺失数字Cantor集（missing-digit Cantor sets）的径向投影与集合差/除集（division sets）问题。证明了当c_a+c_b≥1时，任何观察点对K_{a,m}×K_{b,n}的径向投影都有非空内部；当基是乘性独立且c_a+c_b<1时，构造了显式无界开放观察者集合使其径向像紧致且无处稠密。因此c_a+c_b=1是该"全观察者内部性质"的精确阈值。这为Yu的两个猜想提供了显式两集合反例。文中还建立了仿射平移的模相位障碍充分条件，并结合Banaji-Yu的固定支撑正测度定理构造了紧致、完全、正Lebesgue测度但无处稠密的连续块除集。最后证明了在足够大的r和s下，两个自乘积包含区间，而交叉除集仍无处稠密。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在精确刻画缺失数字Cantor集的径向投影何时对一切观察者具有非空内部性质，并检验和反驳Yu提出的两个关于非空内部结论的猜想。通过建立c_a+c_b=1这一精确阈值，研究者希望系统性地理解丢失数字Cantor集的乘积在仿射投影和差分/除集操作下的几何与测度结构，填补该领域关于乘性依赖与乘性独立情形差异的理解空白。

Method: 本文采用以下技术路线：(1) 利用c_a=m/(a-1)与c_b=n/(b-1)作为维度参数的组合条件，证明c_a+c_b≥1时径向投影含内点（无需乘性独立假设）；(2) 在乘性独立条件下，当c_a+c_b<1时通过显式构造无界开放观察者区域，展示径向像的紧致无处稠密性；(3) 建立模相位障碍（modular phase obstruction）的充分条件来分析仿射平移的相交结构；(4) 结合Banaji-Yu的固定支撑正测度定理构造紧致、完全、正测度、无处稠密的连续块除集；(5) 运用Fourier l¹维数估计及Yu乘积定理中的incidence论证研究自乘积与交叉除集的区间包含性与无处稠密性。

Result: 论文获得以下主要结果：(1) c_a+c_b≥1时，径向投影从任何观察点出发都有非空内部，无需乘性独立假设；(2) 当基乘性独立且c_a+c_b<1时，存在显式无界开放观察者集使径向像紧致无处稠密，有理观察者具有此性质者在每个外角区域稠密且可任意接近四角；(3) 由此确定c_a+c_b=1为该族全观察者内部性质的精确阈值，并给出Yu两个猜想的显式两集合反例；(4) 建立了模相位障碍的充分条件；(5) 结合Banaji-Yu正测度定理构造了紧致、完全、正Lebesgue测度且无处稠密的连续块除集，两因子维数趋于1；(6) 对足够大的r和s，两个自乘积包含区间而交叉除集仍无处稠密。

Conclusion: 该工作完整确定了缺失数字Cantor集（初始连续数字块）乘积径向投影的"全观察者内部性质"的确切阈值c_a+c_b=1，在乘性独立框架内彻底解决了该问题，同时反驳了Yu的两个猜想。所建立的模相位障碍方法与构造的无处稠密正测度连续块除集，为理解自相似集在仿射变换与除集操作下的精细结构开辟了新途径。Fourier维数估计与incidence论证的联合使用也展示了这两个工具在同类问题中的适用性与极限。

Abstract: Let $K_{a,m}$ and $K_{b,n}$ be missing-digit Cantor sets with initial consecutive digit sets, and write $c_a=m/(a-1)$ and $c_b=n/(b-1)$. We prove that if $c_a+c_b\geq 1$, then the radial projection of $K_{a,m}\times K_{b,n}$ from every observer has nonempty interior; no multiplicative-independence assumption is needed for this implication. Conversely, when the bases are multiplicatively independent and $c_a+c_b<1$, we exhibit explicit unbounded open sets of observers for which the radial image is compact and nowhere dense. Rational observers with the same property are dense in each exterior corner region and occur arbitrarily close to the four corners of the product. Thus $c_a+c_b=1$ is the exact threshold for the all-observers interior property within the multiplicatively independent initial-block family. In particular, this supplies an explicit two-set counterexample to the nonempty-interior conclusions of two conjectures of Yu. We also establish a sufficient modular phase obstruction for affine translates of such sets. Combining it with a fixed-pin positive-measure theorem of Banaji and Yu yields consecutive-block division sets that are compact, perfect, of positive Lebesgue measure, and nowhere dense, with both factor dimensions tending to one. For the same family, Fourier $l^1$-dimension estimates and the incidence argument used in Yu's product theorem imply that the two self-products contain intervals for all sufficiently large $r$ and $s$, while the cross-division set remains nowhere dense.

</details>


### [5] [Sparse bounds for maximal rough singular integrals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03751)
*Yuhao Wu*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 本文研究粗糙齐次奇异积分算子最大截断T_Ω^*的定量不对称稀疏界。对L^∞角核，对任意1<p<∞建立(p')^4阶稀疏界；对角核属于L^{q,1}log L时对q'≤p<∞同样成立，第一项为真正的L^1平均。这改善了先前对称与Orlicz-bumped稀疏界，并将最大稀疏理论推广到无界角核。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前对粗糙齐次奇异积分最大截断T_Ω^*的稀疏界是对称或Orlicz-bumped类型，其平均结构不够精细；且现有最大稀疏理论仅限于有界角核情形。作者旨在建立第一项为真正L^1平均的不对称稀疏界，并将该结果推广至无界角核，以获得更优的算子范数估计。

Method: 证明方法结合了四项关键技术：(1) 局部化稀疏控制原理；(2) 最大截断的物理空间线性化；(3) Rademacher-Menshov不等式（用于处理最大截断的随机化求和）；(4) 粗糙核的微局部分解（即对核的频率/角结构进行局部化处理）。通过这些工具对最大算子进行逐步稀疏控制。

Result: 对Ω∈L^∞(S^{d-1})，对所有1<p<∞得到：‖T_Ω^*‖_{(1,p)-稀疏} ≲_d (p')^4‖Ω‖_{L^∞}。对无界角核，若1<q<∞且Ω∈L^{q,1}log L(S^{d-1})，则q'≤p<∞时同样的稀疏界以C_{d,q}(p')^4‖Ω‖_{L^{q,1}log L}成立。稀疏形式的第一项为真正的L^1平均。

Conclusion: 该结果将最大粗糙奇异积分截断的稀疏理论从有界角核推广到无界角核（L^{q,1}log L），给出了定量最优的(p')^4增长阶，并改善了稀疏形式的平均结构（第一个分量取L^1而非对称/Orlicz-bumped平均），为进一步研究该算子族的加权不等式与点态收敛性质奠定了基础。

Abstract: Let $Ω\in L^1(S^{d-1})$ have vanishing average, and let $T_Ω^\ast$ be the maximal truncation of the associated rough homogeneous singular integral. We prove quantitative asymmetric sparse bounds for $T_Ω^\ast$. If $Ω\in L^\infty(S^{d-1})$, then, for every $1<p<\infty$, \[
  \|T_Ω^\ast\|_{(1,p)\text{-}\mathrm{sparse}}
  \lesssim_d
  (p')^4\|Ω\|_{L^\infty(S^{d-1})}. \] For unbounded angular kernels, if $1<q<\infty$ and $Ω\in L^{q,1}\log L(S^{d-1})$, then the same estimate holds for $q'\leq p<\infty$, with the right-hand side replaced by \[
  C_{d,q}(p')^4
  \|Ω\|_{L^{q,1}\log L(S^{d-1})}. \] Thus the first entry of the sparse form is a genuine $L^1$ average. This improves the averaging structure of the previously known symmetric and Orlicz-bumped sparse bounds for maximal rough truncations and extends the maximal sparse theory to unbounded angular kernels. The proof combines a localized sparse domination principle with a physical-space linearization of the maximal truncation, the Rademacher-Menshov inequality, and a microlocal decomposition of the rough kernel.

</details>


### [6] [Hausdorff--Choquet Angular Spaces and Weak-Type $(1,1)$ Bounds for Rough Maximal Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03785)
*Yanping Chen,Zhengyang Ji*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 本文研究了Stein提出的长期未解猜想：当Ω仅属于L¹(球面)时，极大算子M_Ω是否具有弱(1,1)型有界性。作者在Ω属于新空间X(球面)（与Hausdorff-Choquet角空间相关，严格介于L log⁺L与L¹之间）的情况下部分解决了该猜想，证明了弱(1,1)型有界性，显著改进了Christ和Rubio de Francia的结果。此外，对于一般Hausdorff-Choquet尺度HC_α，作者证明α=(n-1)/2是获得一致弱(1,1)型估计的尖锐指数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Stein提出的关于粗糙核极大算子M_Ω在Ω仅满足L¹可积性条件下的弱(1,1)型有界性猜想是调和分析中长期未解决的重要开放问题。该问题关乎极大算子的有界性理论在极弱正则条件下的边界行为，此前Christ和Rubio de Francia的工作给出了部分结果，但仍存在明显的差距需要改进。

Method: 作者引入与Hausdorff-Choquet角空间相关的新函数空间X(S^{n-1})，该空间严格包含L log⁺L并严格包含在L¹中。通过对该空间中Ω的结构分析以及Hausdorff-Choquet角尺度的精细刻画，作者证明了M_Ω在这些条件下满足弱(1,1)型估计，并通过构造论证确定了均匀弱(1,1)估计的尖锐指数α=(n-1)/2。

Result: 1) 在Ω∈X(S^{n-1})条件下证明了M_Ω的弱(1,1)型有界性，其中L log⁺L ⊊ X ⊊ L¹，显著改进了Christ和Rubio de Francia的先前结果；2) 对于Hausdorff-Choquet尺度HC_α，证明了α=(n-1)/2是均匀弱(1,1)型估计成立的尖锐（最优）指数。

Conclusion: 该工作部分解决了Stein的开放猜想，将弱(1,1)型有界性从L log⁺L范畴推广到了一个更大但严格包含于L¹的空间X中，并确定了Hausdorff-Choquet框架下均匀弱(1,1)估计的最优指数，为最终完全解决Stein猜想提供了重要的方法和路径。

Abstract: In the present paper, we consider the maximal operator
  \[
  \mathcal M_Ωf(x)
  :=
  \sup_{r>0}\frac{1}{r^n}
  \int_{|y|<r}
  |f(x-y)|
  \left|
  Ω\!\left(\frac{y}{|y|}\right)
  \right|\,dy.
  \]
  A longstanding open conjecture raised by E.~M.~Stein asks whether the maximal operator $\mathcal M_Ω$ is of weak type $(1,1)$ when $Ω$ is merely in $L^1(\mathbb S^{n-1})$. We partially settle this problem by proving weak type $(1,1)$ bounds of $\mathcal M_Ω$ with kernel $Ω\in\mathcal X(\mathbb S^{n-1})$, yielding a significant improvement over the work of M.~Christ and Rubio de Francia. Here $\mathcal X(\mathbb S^{n-1})$ is the space related to the Hausdorff--Choquet angular space and $$
  L\log^+\!L(\mathbb S^{n-1})
  \subsetneq
  \mathcal X(\mathbb S^{n-1})\subset L^1(\mathbb S^{n-1}).
  $$
  Finally, for the general Hausdorff--Choquet scale $\mathcal H\mathcal C_α$, we show that $α=(n-1)/2$ is the sharp exponent for uniform weak type $(1,1)$ estimates.

</details>


### [7] [Endpoint Estimates for Stein's Purely Quadratic Carleson Operator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04101)
*Anastasios Fragkos,Ben Krause,Michael Lacey*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 本文研究了极大二次调制Hilbert变换及其lacunary版本在L^1附近的函数空间性质。结果表明，若Young函数Φ(t)=o(t log₂t)，则算子不满足Φ-模估计，尤其不是弱(1,1)型；正面地，建立了全算子的L log₁L模估计和lacunary算子的L(log₂L)²log₄L模估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Carleson算子的二次调制类比（即二次Chirp调制Hilbert变换）在调和分析中具有重要意义。理解这类极大算子在有界变差类或L^1空间的端点行为是一个核心问题，特别是确定其弱(1,1)型是否成立以及精确的Orlicz模估计。

Method: 通过构造适当的检验函数或利用反例来证明Φ-模估计的否定结果（当Φ(t)=o(t log₂t)时），并利用调和分析的现有工具如时间频率分析、分解技术和Orlicz范数估计来建立正面的模估计。

In particular, neither the lacunary nor the full quadratic operator is of weak type $(1,1)$. </details>

Abstract: Clouâtre, Ostermann, and Ransford formulated an abstract version of Crouzeix's conjecture involving a bounded unital homomorphism from a uniform algebra into matrices and a unital antilinear map. We also prove that the weakly Fatou (we propose the term ``demicontinuous'') normed lattices are precisely the ones isomorphic to regular sublattices of monotonically complete Banach lattices. In order to do so we introduce the concept of the Lorentz completion of a demicontinuous normed lattice, which is somewhat analogous to the universal completion from the vector lattice theory. Furthermore, we unify and simplify the proofs of the characterizations of monotone completeness from \cite{aw} and \cite{taylor} and provide their quantitative versions.
  The semicontinuity-related properties in AM-spaces have some additional features. While the classical Kakutani theorem states that AM-spaces are precisely the closed sublattices of $\Co\left(K\right)$-spaces, we show, using two different methods, that the semicontinuous AM-spaces are precisely the closed regular sublattices of $\Co\left(K\right)$-spaces. Finally, we prove that for a normed space $E$, the AM-space of positively homogeneous weak* continuous functions on $\Ba_{E^{*}}$ is semicontinuous iff it is a regular sublattice of $\Co\left(\Ba_{E^{*}},\mathrm{w}^{*}\right)$ iff $\dim E<\8$.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: For positive integers $n\ge r$, let $λ(r,n)$ denote the \emph{maximal relative projection constant} of $r$-dimensional subspaces of $\ell_\infty^n$ and $λ(r)$ denote the \emph{maximal absolute projection constant}, respectively. It is known that for any fixed $r$, $λ(r,n)$ is a non-decreasing sequence with limit $λ(r)$ as $n\to \infty$. A natural question is whether $λ(r,n)$ stabilizes at $λ(r)$ for some $n>r$. We prove that for any fixed $r$, \[λ(r,n)=λ(r) \qquad \text{for every}\qquad n\ge 2^{r}\binom{r+1}{2}.\] This answers a question of Basso. The technique used is of independent interest.

Abstract: We study the Almost-Invariant Subspace Problem for operators defined on non-normable Fréchet spaces and especially on $ω$. Abstract: We prove the following inequality for Dirichlet polynomials: \[ \int_0^1 |f(1/2+σ)|\,dσ\lesssim \lim_{T\to\infty} \frac{1}{2T} \int_{-T}^T |f(it)| \, dt. \] In particular, for a Dirichlet series $f(s) = \sum_{n\geq 1} a_n n^{-s}$ belonging to the Hardy space $\mathscr{H}^1$ of Dirichlet series, \[
  \left|a_1+\sum_{n=2}^\infty \frac{a_n}{\sqrt n\log n}\right|
  \lesssim \|f\|_{\mathscr{H}^1}. \] This answers a question raised previously in the literature and it proves that the multiplicative Hilbert matrix has a bounded symbol.

</details>

Abstract: The aim of this paper is to develop a framework for measure theory that avoids infinities and allows for the uniform treatment of positive and vector measures. Our approach is based on a modification of the notion of measure, which supplements the usual $σ$-additivity requirement with a suitable maximality condition. To each Hausdorff topological vector space $\mathfrak A$ and $σ$-ring $\mathcal{Q}$, we associate a vector space $\mathscr M(\mathcal{Q},\mathfrak A)$ of `infinite' $\mathfrak A$-valued measures corresponding to $\mathcal{Q}$. In particular, the positive elements of $\mathscr M(\mathcal{Q},\mathbb R)$ are naturally identified with the $σ$-finite positive measures defined on $\mathcal{Q}$, thus placing positive and signed measures within the same setting. Finally, extension results for group-valued contents due to Sion and Weber are reformulated and refined within this new framework.

Abstract: Motivated by the weak maximizing property ($\mathrm{WMP}$), we investigate $p$-summability conditions on maximizing sequences of bounded linear operators. Since the naive $p$-summability formulation is independent of $p$ and collapses to norm attainment for all operators, we introduce the weakly $p$-singular maximizing property ($\mathrm{SMP}_p$), based on maximizing sequences with no weakly $p$-summable subsequence. We completely characterize when the pairs $(\ell_p,\ell_q)$ and $(\ell_p,c_0)$ have the $\mathrm{SMP}_r$, revealing sharp contrasts with the $\mathrm{WMP}$. We also characterize the Schur property of order $p$ via the $\mathrm{WMP}$ and the $\mathrm{SMP}_r$. Under relative weak $p$-precompactness and suitable Dunford-Pettis-type assumptions, we characterize universal norm attainment for operators from $X$ to $Y$ in terms of either the $\mathrm{SMP}_q$ for adjoint operators or the weak$^{*}$-to-weak$^{*}$ maximizing property, and derive corresponding duality consequences for the $\mathrm{SMP}_p$. Finally, we introduce $p$-convergent perturbation properties for operators and their adjoints, characterize them for classical sequence spaces, and show that the $p$-convergent perturbation property is strictly weaker than the $\mathrm{SMP}_p$.

Abstract: Let $1<p<2$ and $D_j$ be the discrete partial derivative on the Hamming cube $Ω_n = \{ -1, 1\}^n$. Abstract: We determine the growth scale of the optimal constants in the real anisotropic Bohnenblust--Hille inequality. For an exponent vector $\mathbf q^{(m)}$, write $C_{\mathbf q^{(m)}}^{(m)}$ for its optimal constant and $d_m$ for its diameter. These constants are superpolynomial precisely when $m d_m/\log m\to\infty$; throughout this regime, their logarithm has the sharp scale $m d_m$. If also $d_m\to0$, then $\log C_{\mathbf q^{(m)}}^{(m)}/(m d_m)$ lies asymptotically in the interval $\left[\frac{\log 2}{4},\frac{2-\log 2-γ}{4}\right]$, where $γ$ is the Euler--Mascheroni constant; this interval has width less than $10^{-2}$. We also solve the extremal problems at fixed diameter and fixed total deficit. The normalized reciprocal-deficit profiles order the canonically arranged optimal constants by majorization, yield exact formulas on a full-dimensional region, and recover the lower coefficient $(\log 2)/4$ throughout a broad class of anisotropic regimes.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文研究集合正整数的"可容许排列"问题（避免按位置顺序出现的递增或递减三项等差数列）。作者聚焦于二分Dyadic候选集S_A（偶数块k≥2的并集），证明其不存在可容许排列，从而说明该典型二分构造不能解决Davis等人提出的"两集合问题"。主要方法是将可容许性归约为一种次序gadget问题，并通过对8模同余的M证明一个三约束核心的不一致性来完成证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Davis等人证明了正整数不能被可容许排列（即无法将所有正整数排列成不含位置顺序上单调三项等差数列的序列），同时将它们划分成三个可容许排列的集合，并提出了"两个集合是否足够"的开放问题。本文旨在验证一个自然的典型候选（二分Dyadic分解）能否解决这一两集合问题。

Method: 研究S_A = ∪_{k≥2, k偶} (2^{k-1}, 2^k]块的Dyadic结构。建立了多项结构限制：轨道阻碍（orbit obstruction）、非对称平衡律（asymmetric balance law）以及大Dyadic块不能作为连续段排列的性质。核心方法是构造归约：将S_A的可容许性约化为(M, 2M]上序gadget的可行性问题，要求避免单调三项等差数列且满足由15和16诱导的十五个优先级约束。随后证明该gadget的一个三约束核心在每个M≡0 (mod 8)时不成立，使用之字形传播、传递锁和镜像洪泛归纳法证明。

Result: 证明了对所有M≡0 (mod 8)的情况，gadget的三约束核心不一致，且由于每个相关Dyadic尺度均落在此同余类中，S_A不存在可容许排列。因此典型二分Dyadic划分并不能解决两集合问题，该问题仍然开放。独立SAT编码与证书检查对证明提供了辅助验证。

Conclusion: S_A不是可容许排列的，排除了一个自然的两集合候选解。这推进了对Davis等人提出的两集合问题的理解，显示该开放问题需要更精细的构造，而非简单的二分Dyadic分块方案。人类可读的证明已通过SAT编码得到辅助验证。

Abstract: Call a permutation of a set of positive integers admissible if it contains no increasing or decreasing three-term arithmetic progression in position order. Davis, Entringer, Graham, and Simmons proved that the positive integers are not admissibly permutable, partitioned them into three admissibly permutable sets, and asked whether two sets suffice. We study the canonical dyadic candidate S_A, the union of the blocks (2^{k-1}, 2^k] over even k >= 2. We establish several structural restrictions on admissible permutations, including an orbit obstruction, an asymmetric balance law, and the impossibility of placing all sufficiently large dyadic blocks as contiguous runs. For the main result, we reduce admissibility of S_A to feasibility of an order gadget on (M, 2M]: the order must avoid monotone three-term progressions and satisfy fifteen precedence constraints induced by the values 15 and 16. We then prove that a three-constraint core of this gadget is inconsistent for every M congruent to 0 mod 8, using zigzag propagation on arithmetic-progression ladders, a transfer lock, and mirror-flood induction. Since every relevant dyadic scale lies in this residue class, S_A admits no admissible permutation. Thus the canonical dyadic partition does not solve the two-set problem, which remains open. Independent SAT encodings and certificate checks provide auxiliary verification of the human-readable proof.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Alon证明了f(d)=2^d对所有充分大的d成立，并留有一个开放问题：该等式是否对所有d≥4成立。填补这一理论空白，确保连通图码理论的完整性，是该论文的核心动机。

Method: 作者对每个d≥4构造无穷多个有限简单d-正则二分图携带维度为d的线性连通图码。构造始于K_{d,d}的一个向量标记拷贝。对于d≥7，所需标记通过对GL_d(2)中一个不可约共轭类进行概率计数获得；对于d=4,5,6，使用可由精确穷举程序验证的显式矩阵。随后采用循环电压提升技术产生所需的无穷族。

Result: 对所有d≥4，f(d)=2^d，即对每个d≥4存在无穷多个两两不同构的d-正则图H满足m(H)=2^d，从而肯定地回答了Alon的猜想。

Conclusion: 本文完全解决了Alon关于连通图码最大尺寸的猜想，证明对所有d≥4均有f(d)=2^d，将原证明从"充分大的d"推广到全部d≥4，并通过电压图技术给出了系统性构造方法。

Abstract: For a finite graph $H$, a connectivity graph-code is a family $\mathcal C\subseteq 2^{E(H)}$ such that $A\triangle B$ is a connected spanning subgraph of $H$ whenever $A$ and $B$ are distinct members of $\mathcal C$. Let $m(H)$ denote the maximum size of such a family, and let $f(d)$ be the largest integer $q$ for which $m(H)=q$ for infinitely many pairwise nonisomorphic $d$-regular graphs $H$. Restricting codewords to the edges incident with a vertex gives $f(d)\leq 2^d$. Alon proved equality for all sufficiently large $d$ and asked whether it holds for every $d\geq 4$. We answer this question affirmatively. More precisely, for every $d\geq 4$ we construct infinitely many finite simple $d$-regular bipartite graphs carrying a linear connectivity graph-code of dimension $d$. The construction begins with a vector-labelled copy of $K_{d,d}$. For $d\geq 7$, the required labelling follows from a probabilistic count over an irreducible conjugacy class in $\mathrm{GL}_d(2)$; explicit matrices, verified by a short exact exhaustive program, cover $d=4,5,6$. Cyclic voltage lifts then produce the required infinite families.

</details>

Abstract: We introduce the notion of a non-attacking rook placement on a crossword grid. A crossword grid is a collection of white squares (which comprise across and down words) and black squares (which separate the words), and a complete non-attacking rook placement on such a grid is a subset of white squares which intersects every across and every down word exactly once. We prove an upper bound on the number of rook placements that a general grid can admit. Abstract: An edge-colored graph is \emph{rainbow} if all of its edges receive distinct colors. For a fixed graph $H$, an edge-colored graph $F$ is called weakly $H$-rainbow saturated if there exists an ordering $e_1,e_2,\ldots,e_{|E(\bar{F})|}$ of $E(\bar{F})$ such that, for any edge coloring $c$ of $E(\bar{F})$ with $c(e_i)\neq c(e_j)$, there is always a rainbow copy of $H$ that contains $e_i$ in $F+\{e_1,e_2,\ldots,e_i\}$. Thus, their dependence on $\ell$ is linear for paths and quadratic for stars. We then focus on cycles. We answer this question negatively by giving an explicit construction showing that, for every $\ell\ge4$ and all sufficiently large $n$, $$\s(n,C_\ell)< \frac{\ell}{\ell-1}n+c_\ell,$$ where $c_\ell$ depends only on $\ell$. Since $\frac{\ell}{\ell-1}<\frac32$, this strictly improves the proposed leading coefficient for every cycle $C_\ell$ with $\ell\ge4$.

Abstract: Let $G$ be a graph of order $n$, and let $s^+(G)$ and $s^-(G)$ denote the sums of the squares of the positive and negative adjacency eigenvalues of $G$, respectively. Recently, Liu, Tang, and Zhang proved the conjecture of Elphick, Farber, Goldberg, and Wocjan that every connected graph $G$ of order $n$ satisfies $
\min\{s^+(G), s^-(G)\} \ge n-1. $
For positive square energy, we strengthen this result by showing that every $2$-connected graph $G$ of order $n$ which is not a cycle satisfies $s^+(G)\ge n$. The formally analogous assertion for $s^-$ is false: the complete graph $K_n$ satisfies $s^-(K_n)=n-1$. We prove a natural counterpart in the triangle-free class: every triangle-free $2$-connected noncycle $G$ satisfies $
  \min\{s^+(G),s^-(G)\}>n. $
More generally, it is enough that some maximum-degree vertex of $G$ belongs to no triangle. Together with the exact square energies of cycles, this characterizes the triangle-free $2$-connected graphs for which $s^-(G)\ge n$; the only exceptions are the cycles $C_{4k+3}$ with $k\geq1$.

Abstract: We consider an alternative notion of minimality for C*-dynamical systems, which we refer to as strong minimality. This notion coincides with the usual definition of minimality in the commutative setting, but diverges sharply from it in the noncommutative setting in a way which we argue addresses several fundamental defects in the usual definition. We demonstrate that strong minimality is deeply connected with Rørdam's notion of a C*-irreducible inclusion, giving a new perspective on the study of such inclusions. We provide various characterizations of this property, including for reduced crossed products, tensor products, and subhomogeneous C*-algebras.

</details>

Here two more are offered and comparisons are made to the recent work of Her and Zhong defining an invariant of a path of symplectic matrices motivated by earlier work of Lidskii and Gelfand, Salomon and Zehnder.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解Kähler-Ricci流在紧流形上的有限时间奇点类型（Type I vs Type II）及其极限模型是几何分析的核心问题。作者此前在"two-bolt"情形中完成了相关分析，本文旨在将该分析推广到更微妙、更复杂的"nut-bolt"与"two-nut"情形，从而完整刻画由圆丛叶理构造的所有流形族上的Kähler-Ricci流奇点行为，并确定流奇点的精确Cheeger-Gromov极限模型。

Method: 本文严格分析了由N = N_1 × ⋯ × N_r上的圆丛1-参数叶理所构造的流形上的Kähler-Ricci流。采用DW2011和WW等工作中使用的圆丛ansatz构造度量。"nut-bolt"情形下，区间一端nut型坍缩（N' = N_2 × ⋯ × N_r）另一端bolt坍缩（N），紧化\hat{M}为N'上的ℂP^{m+1}丛；"two-nut"情形下两端均nut型坍缩，紧化\hat{M}为∏_{k≥3}N_k上的ℂP^{m_0+m_ℓ+1}丛。通过分析三种情形下奇点的Type I性质，并对流的重缩放与膨胀序列做pointed Cheeger-Gromov极限研究，证明极限模型为乘积形式，其中Σ为ℂP^{m+1}、Tot(𝒪^{⊕(m+1)})或投射化丛三种之一，且度量满足Kähler-Ricci shrinker的圆丛ansatz。

Result: 论文证明了：1) 在"two-bolt"、"nut-bolt"与"two-nut"所有三种情形下，Kähler-Ricci流的奇点必为Type I型；2) 三种情形下重缩放和膨胀序列的pointed Cheeger-Gromov极限模型必须为(Σ^{m+1}, g_Σ(t)) × (ℂ^k, flat)（m,k ≥ 0），其中Σ只能是三类几何对象之一：ℂP^{m+1}、全空间Tot(𝒪^{⊕(m+1)})、或投射化ℙ(𝒪^{⊕(m_0+1)} ⊕ 𝒪^{⊕(m_ℓ+1)})（m_0+m_ℓ=m），𝒪为N_1,...,N_r中部分因子的线丛；极限度量g_Σ(t)为满足圆丛ansatz的Kähler-Ricci shrinker。

Conclusion: 作者完整刻画了由圆丛1-参数叶理在Kähler-Einstein流形乘积上构造的三类流形（two-bolt、nut-bolt、two-nut）上Kähler-Ricci流的奇点行为：所有奇点均为Type I型，且极限模型完全分类为三类具体几何对象的（附加平坦因子的）乘积，其中度量是满足相应ansatz的Kähler-Ricci shrinker。这一结果系统推广并完善了此前two-bolt情形的结论，为理解Kähler-Ricci流奇点的Typing分类与极限模型提供了重要进展。

Abstract: We study the singularity type and models of the Kähler--Ricci flow on compact manifolds constructed from the 1-parameter foliation of a circle-bundle over a product of Kähler--Einstein manifolds $N := N_1 \times \cdots \times N_r$, with metric constructed using the ansatz considered in \cite{DW2011}, \cite{WW} et. al.
  In the earlier work \cite{FT} by the authors, we considered the ``two-bolt'' case where both ends of the foliation close with the ``bolt'' $N$. In this article, we continue our work on the more subtle ``nut-bolt'' and ``two-nut'' cases. The former has one end of the interval closes with a nut-type collapse (i.e. $N' := N_2 \times \cdots \times N_r$) and the other with a bolt (i.e. $N$). The compactification $\widehat{M}$ is then a $\mathbb{CP}^{m+1}$-bundle over $N'$. The ``two-nut'' case is one that both ends close with nut-type collapses, necessarily two of the $N_i$'s must be $\mathbb{CP}^{m_0}$ and $\mathbb{CP}^{m_\ell}$, and the compactification $\widehat{M}$ is a $\mathbb{CP}^{m_0+m_\ell+1}$-bundle over $\prod_{k\geq 3}N_k$. We proved that in all ``two-bolt'', ``nut-bolt'' and ``two-nut'' caess the singularity must be of Type I.
  Furthermore, we study the pointed Cheeger-Gromov limit of the rescaled and dilated sequence of the flow in all of three cases, and prove that the limit model must be $(Σ^{m+1}, g_Σ(t)) \times (\mathbb{C}^{k}, \textrm{flat})$ with $m, k \geq 0$, where $Σ$ is one of the following: $\mathbb{CP}^{m+1}$, $\textrm{Tot}(\mathcal{L}^{\oplus(m+1)})$, or a projectivization $\mathbb{P}\big(\mathcal{O}^{\oplus(m_0+1)} \oplus \mathcal{L}^{\oplus(m_\ell+1)}\big)$ with $m_0 + m_\ell = m$, and $\mathcal{L}$ is a line bundle over the product of \emph{some} of the $N_1, \cdots, N_r$ factors. The metric $g_Σ(t)$ is a Kähler-Ricci shrinker satisfying the circle-bundle ansatz.

</details>

Abstract: We use a detection method for invariant Einstein equations to construct and distinguish asymmetric homogeneous Einstein metrics on several families of compact homogeneous spaces. The four-factor case exhibits a further phenomenon. On \[
  M_9=\SU(18)^4/Δ\Sp(9) \] the two symmetry types $3+1$ and $2+2$ are both realized: up to homothety and internal block permutations, the corresponding fixed families contain exactly three asymmetric Einstein metrics, two with three equal horizontal scales and one distinct scale (type $3+1$), and one with two equal pairs of horizontal scales (type $2+2$). They are pairwise non-isometric, Riemannian irreducible, and not naturally reductive. The $2+2$ metric is globally unique in the full positive $2+2$ fixed family up to block interchange.

Abstract: This note originated with a mini-course taught at KU Leuven by the author in June 2026. We review the definition of weightings (or quasi-homogeneous structures), the weighted normal bundle, and the weighted deformation space. We emphasize the algebraic approach and functorial properties, using sheaves of ideals and the character spectrum for sheaves of algebras. We review the definition of a multiplicative weighting on a Lie groupoid $G\rightrightarrows M$, and give a self-contained proof of the three-way equivalence between (i) multiplicative weightings of $G$ along $M$, (ii) Lie filtrations of the Lie algebroid $A$ of $G$, (iii) dg weightings of the NQ manifold $A[1]$ along $M$. The corresponding weighted deformation space is a weighted variant of the tangent/adiabatic groupoid and is used in the study of hypoelliptic operators.

Abstract: Colding established a sharp gradient estimate for the Green function on manifolds with nonnegative Ricci curvature, which was extended to positive Ricci curvature by Manea recently. We prove almost rigidity of such gradient estimates for positive Ricci curvature. We show that the average of the gradient deficit, namely $\fint (1-|\nabla b|^2)\,dV$, controls the volume deficit in a quantitative manner; here $b$ is the distance-like function defined using the Green function. This implies a quantitative almost rigidity theorem for manifolds with $\mathrm{Ric}\ge(n-1)g$ and a gap theorem for Einstein manifolds. We also prove a rigidity theorem for closed 4-dimensional Einstein manifolds by finding a new monotonicity formula.

</details>

Abstract: We introduce a new approach to the study of Bochner-Kähler manifolds, based on normal form techniques in CR geometry. As observed by Webster, Bochner-Kähler manifolds are intimately related to spherical Sasakian manifolds. Our approach utilises this relationship as well as normal forms for spherical Sasakian manifolds (known as rigid spheres). In this setting potentials of Bochner-Kähler manifolds are nothing but the defining equations of rigid spheres in rigid normal form.
  This leads to a canonical formula for Bochner-Kähler potentials, as well as streamlined proofs of known results on the structure and the symmetries of Bochner-Kähler manifolds. Our approach also provides a host of examples in terms of an implicit formula of the corresponding Kähler potentials.

Abstract: We develop a geometric framework for conservative mechanical systems modeled on conic pseudo-Finslerian manifolds, extending classical Riemannian and semi-Riemannian mechanics to anisotropic geometries. In this setting, we define a Jacobi-type pseudo-Finslerian metric and demonstrate that motions of fixed energy correspond, up to reparametrization, to its geodesics. We establish energy and momentum conservation under suitable symmetry assumptions via a generalized Noether's theorem, and provide new analytical criteria for the completeness of both the Jacobi metric and the mechanical system. Furthermore, for mechanical systems with holonomic and non-holonomic constraints, we prove the existence and uniqueness of reaction forces satisfying d'Alembert's principle. These results provide a unified geometric foundation for constrained and unconstrained anisotropic dynamics.

</details>

Method: 作者从概念层面推广了半黎曼浸没与叶状结构的定义，允许叶层具有变化或退化的因果特征。关键方法包括：(1) 引入广义半黎曼叶状结构并证明与transnormal性质的等价条件（当水平分布是基本的）；(2) 引入stationary叶状结构，并将其与共形观察者的空间余空间上的经典黎曼叶状结构建立联系；(3) 利用这些工具在特定时空（正曲率RW时空、static时空）中获得刚性结论；(4) 推导适用于总分布对合子流形的推广O'Neill型截面曲率公式。

Abstract: In this article, we introduce the concepts of generalized semi-Riemannian submersions and foliations, extending the classical framework to accommodate leaves with varying or degenerate causal characters, such as homogeneous foliations on semi-Riemannian manifolds and codimension-one lightlike foliations on Lorentz manifolds (including pp-waves). We establish that a regular foliation with a basic horizontal distribution is generalized semi-Riemannian if and only if it is transnormal. Furthermore, we introduce stationary foliations and prove that a stationary transnormal foliation induces a classical Riemannian foliation on the spatial rest space of a conformal observer. As a geometric consequence, we obtain a rigidity result ruling out stationary codimension-one lightlike foliations on positively curved Robertson-Walker spacetimes or static spacetimes. Finally, we derive an O'Neill-type sectional curvature formula for submersions with an involutive total distribution.

</details>

*Boris Kruglikov*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文解决了经典活动标架法计算微分不变量时产生根式等代数函数，与全局Lie-Tresse定理声称微分不变量代数由有理函数生成之间的矛盾，并给出构造性方法以算法化计算全局微分不变量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典活动标架法在计算群作用的微分不变量时，所得表达式中常常出现根式及更复杂的代数函数；然而全局Lie-Tresse定理表明，对于代数伪群作用，微分不变量代数由有限多个有理函数（在jet变量中）生成。这两者之间存在明显矛盾，需要解释并统一。

Method: 作者通过详细分析几个精心设计的示例，阐明经典运动标架方法中出现的根式/代数函数本质上是局部的、依赖于标架选择的伪不变量表达，而全局Lie-Tresse定理中的有理生成元是在（经过适当降阶后的）jet变量的有理函数域中构造的。文中给出构造性算法，将局部代数表达转化为全局有理不变量的生成。

Result: 解释了经典活动标架法与全局Lie-Tresse定理表面矛盾的内在原因，并通过若干经典示例加以验证和说明；同时提供了一种算法化计算全局微分不变量的构造性方法。

Conclusion: 局部运动标架得到的含根式的微分不变量与全局Lie-Tresse定理的有理生成并不冲突——前者是局部截面选择下的表达，后者是全局有理函数域层面的代数结构描述。本文的构造性结果使得全局微分不变量的自动计算成为可能。

Abstract: The classical technique of moving frames allows to effectively compute differential invariants of group actions. A standard observation is that the expressions of differential invariants contain radicals and more complicated algebraic functions, however the global Lie-Tresse theorem claims that for algebraic pseudogroup actions the algebra of differential invariants is generated by rational functions in jet variables. Here, we explain this apparent contradiction and illustrate it in a few well-elaborated examples. Constructively, our result gives a method to algorithmically compute global differential invariants.

</details>

Abstract: We study the properties of the $dd^Φ$-operator on $8$-dimensional Spin(7)-manifolds with torsion-free Spin(7)-structures $Φ$. These operators were first introduced by Harvey and Lawson (An introduction to potential theory in calibrated geometry. Am.J. Math. 131.4 (2009), arXiv:0710.3920). We prove a Hodge decomposition theorem for the $dd^Φ$-operator and obtain an analogue of the $\partial \bar{\partial}$-lemma in Kähler geometry. Using this, we define Bott--Chern-type cohomologies for Spin(7)-manifolds. We relate the Bott-Chern-type cohomology spaces to the moduli space of torsion-free Spin(7)-structures and calibrated geometry of Spin(7)-manifolds. These relations naturally give rise to the notion of Cayley-positive cones. In the course of proving the results, we state and prove various identities for the exterior derivative and its decompositions into irreducible Spin(7)-representations as well as identities for second order derivatives and Laplacians. The identities we prove are for any Spin(7)-structures and the specialized torsion-free ones are Spin(7)-analogoues of Kähler identities and Bryant--Harvey's identities in the $\mathrm{G}_2$-case (R. Bryant, Some remarks on $\mathrm{G}_2$-structures, Proceedings of the 11th and 12th Gökova geometry-topology conference, arXiv:math/0305124) and are results of independent interest.

</details>

Collectively, our techniques allow us to handle a challenging new solution regime where the crease lacks strict convexity.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 作者建立了上述三个性质之间的逻辑等价链，通过函数分析、广义函数（分布）理论及连续方程弱解的精细分析工具进行证明。对于反例，作者在三维空间中构造了一个紧支撑的有界无散度向量场，通过对粒子流（flow）的时序对称性进行破坏，使得输运方程在前向时间具有唯一性而在后向时间丧失唯一性。

  3) generalized square-integrable (with respect to spatial variables) solutions of the Cauchy problem for the corresponding continuity equation are unique both forward an backward in time.

</details>

Abstract: We disprove McKean's conjecture, which asserts that the entropy dissipation is monotone nonincreasing along solutions, or equivalently that the entropy is convex in time, for the spatially homogeneous Landau-Coulomb equation. Remarkably, the counterexamples are close to equilibrium in relative entropy and standard Sobolev norms. Since the Landau equation arises from the Boltzmann equation in the grazing collisions limit, our counterexample also shows that McKean's conjecture is not true in general for the Boltzmann equation.

Abstract: We prove a scaling-critical regularity criterion involving only one velocity component for finite-energy suitable weak solutions of the three-dimensional incompressible Navier--Stokes equations. Each dyadic block of $u^3$ is retained until it is paired with the vertical scale selected by a one-dimensional backward heat kernel. Low frequencies gain from the slab thickness, high frequencies from transferring the projection to the localized flux and applying an inverse Bernstein estimate, and spatially separated pressure sources from harmonic decay. These mechanisms generate a two-sided $\ell^1$ kernel, converting spatial-frequency summability into summability of physical-scale energy increments. Consequently, we obtain a uniform Type-I local energy bound without a Lorentz refinement in time; compactness and one-component rigidity then exclude singular blow-up limits whose third velocity component vanishes.

</details>

Conclusion: 这两个定理表明各向异性极小曲面和全局极小化者在半空间约束下表现出强刚性，推广了经典结果，并通过新的几何工具（椭圆对数外部图和平面剥离）克服了各向异性积分带来的技术困难。

Abstract: We prove two anisotropic halfspace theorems for uniformly elliptic parametric integrands. </details>

The proof combines a shifted Jacobi inequality for \(b=\log(H+J_0)\), a parallel hypersurface transformation that makes the Newton tensor uniformly elliptic and local boundedness estimate then reduces the pointwise bound to a weighted $L^1$ estimate, which is completed using the Jacobi energy inequality and integration by parts.

### [91] [$L^p$ isomorphisms for the fractional Laplacian on $\mathbb{R}^n$ and their application](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03962)

TL;DR: 本文受 Amrouche-Girault-Giroire (1994) 经典理论启发，发展了分数阶拉普拉斯算子的 L^p 理论：引入带权的分数阶 Sobolev 空间 Λ^{s,p}(ℝ^n)，证明分数阶拉普拉斯算子 (−Δ)^{s/2} 在 s∈(0,2)、p∈(1,∞) 范围内是从 Λ^{s,p}(ℝ^n)/P_{[s−n/p]} 到 L^p(ℝ^n) 的同构，并通过对偶将尺度推广至负阶，建立了算子间的精确复合。核心工具是适配的加权 Hardy 与 Poincaré 不等式，先证明在 Schwartz 类闭包上的同构（因密度本身非平凡），再借助 s-调和整函数为多项式的性质推出密度并完成证明。作为应用，解决了 ℝ^n 上分数阶 Stokes 系统的存在唯一性及显式核与相容条件。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典 Amrouche-Girault-Giroire 理论对 ℝ^n 上 Laplace 方程给出了加权 Sobolev 空间之间的同构刻画，但其框架尚未推广到分数阶情形。作者旨在建立分数阶拉普拉斯算子对应的 L^p 理论，填补这一空白，从而为分数阶椭圆方程及流体系统提供严格的函数空间基础。

Method: 引入带权的分数阶 Sobolev 空间 Λ^{s,p}(ℝ^n)，以 ||(−Δ)^{s/2}u||_{L^p} 为最高阶度量；建立 1≤s<2 情形下通过梯度约化得到的加权 Hardy 和 Poincaré 不等式族；由于这些不等式仅在稠密类上先验成立且 C_c^∞ 在 Λ^{s,p} 中的稠密性本身非平凡，先在 Schwartz 类的闭包上证明同构，再利用两个 s-调和解的差为整函数多项式这一事实将闭包等同于全空间，密度作为副产品推出；通过对偶将理论推广至负阶，实现算子空间的同构映射与精确复合。

Result: 证明了对所有 s∈(0,2)、p∈(1,∞)，映射 (−Δ)^{s/2}: Λ^{s,p}(ℝ^n)/P_{[s−n/p]} → L^p(ℝ^n) 是同构，从而方程 (−Δ)^{s/2}u=f 对任意 f∈L^p(ℝ^n) 可解，解在模一个显式的有限维多项式空间下唯一；建立了加权 Hardy 与 Poincaré 不等式；通过密度将同构从 Schwartz 类闭包推广到整个 Λ^{s,p}；将尺度对偶推广至负阶并保证算子复合精确成立；作为应用，得到了分数阶 Stokes 系统的存在唯一性、显式核及相容条件。

Conclusion: 该工作将经典 Laplace 的加权 Sobolev 同构理论完整推广至分数阶情形，为分数阶偏微分方程提供了自然的空间框架与求解工具，并在分数阶 Stokes 系统上展示了其应用价值。所建立的同构、加权不等式与密度结果构成了一套自洽且可扩展的理论体系。

Abstract: A classical theory of Amrouche, Girault and Giroire (1994) resolves the Laplace equation on $\mathbb{R}^n$ through an isomorphism between weighted Sobolev spaces. Inspired by their framework, we develop the corresponding $L^p$ theory for the fractional Laplacian: we introduce weighted fractional Sobolev spaces $Λ^{s,p}(\mathbb{R}^n)$, gauged at top order by $\|(-\triangle)^{\frac{s}{2}}u\|_{L^p}$, and prove that $(-\triangle)^{\frac{s}{2}}:Λ^{s,p}(\mathbb{R}^n)/\mathcal P_{[s-n/p]}\to L^p(\mathbb{R}^n)$ is an isomorphism for all $s\in(0,2)$ and $p\in(1,\infty)$; thus $(-\triangle)^{\frac{s}{2}}u=f$ is solvable for every $f\in L^p(\mathbb{R}^n)$, uniquely modulo an explicit finite-dimensional space of polynomials. More generally, extending the scale by duality to negative orders, the fractional Laplacians of the appropriate orders map its spaces isomorphically onto one another and compose exactly. A key ingredient is a family of weighted Hardy and Poincaré inequalities adapted to $\|(-\triangle)^{\frac{s}{2}}u\|_{L^p}$, established here for $1\le s<2$ by a reduction to the gradient. Since these inequalities hold a priori only on a dense class, while the density of $C_{\mathrm{c}}^\infty(\mathbb{R}^n)$ in $Λ^{s,p}(\mathbb{R}^n)$ is itself nontrivial, we first prove the isomorphism on the closure of the Schwartz class, which coincides with $Λ^{s,p}(\mathbb{R}^n)$ since the difference of two solutions is an entire $s$-harmonic function, hence a polynomial; the density follows as a by-product. As an application, we obtain existence and uniqueness, with explicit kernels and compatibility conditions, for the fractional Stokes system in $\mathbb{R}^n$.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: 作者确认了对两个边际均为全局Lipschitz扰动的Fathi-Mikulincer-Shenfeld猜想成立，提供了严格的渐近常数说明上下界之间的差距，并推广了Caffarelli收缩定理到更一般的扰动框架。

Abstract: We study dimension-free differential estimates for Brenier maps under first-order perturbations of the two marginals. Caffarelli's contraction theorem yields such estimates when the source and target potentials satisfy a pointwise Hessian comparison. We show that the same conclusion remains true under arbitrary globally Lipschitz perturbations of both marginals, without additional assumptions. In particular, this answers the conjecture due to Fathi, Mikulincer, and Shenfeld.
  More precisely, let $d\ge1$, $L\ge0$, and let $B:\mathbb{R}^d\to\mathbb{R}$ be globally $L$-Lipschitz. We prove that the Brenier map from the standard Gaussian measure $γ_d$ to the probability measure proportional to $\mathrm{e}^{-B}γ_d$ has a globally Lipschitz representative whose bound depends only on $L$. The Brenier potential also belongs to $C^{1,1}(\mathbb{R}^d)$ and satisfies dimension-free two-sided Hessian bounds. Let $\mathfrak{C}(0,L)$ denote the upper-bound constant, then \[ \log\mathfrak{C}(0,L)=4L^2+\log L+\mathcal{O}(1) \qquad(L\to\infty), \] and a one-dimensional example with Brenier map $T_L$ satisfies $\log\operatorname{Lip}(T_L)\ge L^2/2$.
  We deduce this Gaussian estimate from an anisotropic two-marginal result. Let $V,W:\mathbb{R}^d\to\mathbb{R}$ be potentials, and let $Q,P$ be positive-definite matrices. Using the distributional curvature bounds $D^2V\preceq Q$ and $D^2W\succeq P$, we establish matrix Hessian bounds for Brenier maps between arbitrary globally Lipschitz perturbations of the reference marginals. Most importantly, the constants keep the directional geometry of the essential gradient ranges. For affine perturbations, our estimates recover the sharp noncommuting Caffarelli tensor. We further obtain pointwise displacement bounds for Gaussian perturbations and spectral Hessian estimates for compact Gaussian mixtures.

</details>


### [94] [Smooth autonomous fast dynamo action on the three-torus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04153)
*Michele Coti Zelati,Massimo Sorella,David Villringer*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文构造了一族光滑、自治、无散的速度场，构成非空$C^k$-开集，在$\mathbb{T}^3$上产生快速发电机，从而解决了Zeldovich和Sakharov提出的Fast Dynamo猜想（记录于Arnold问题集中）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 快速发电机（fast dynamo）是磁流体动力学中的核心问题，涉及磁场在导电流体运动下能否指数增长而不依赖于磁扩散系数。Zeldovich和Sakharov的猜想断言存在光滑自治速度场产生快速发电机，但长期以来缺乏严格构造，尤其是在自治框架下的构造。

Method: 论文采用两步构造策略：(1) 通过拉伸-折叠-剪切（stretch-fold-shear）机制构造一族光滑、时间周期快速发电机，该方法适用于一大类速度场；(2) 利用全局Poincaré截面与适当设定的回归时间函数，将周期动力学嵌入光滑自治流中。在两种情形下，证明先在分布的各向异性Banach空间中分离理想感应算子（ideal induction operator）的不稳定分布特征模，再证明该谱不稳定性在奇异扰动$\varepsilon\Delta$下持久存在。

Result: 获得的估计在$\varepsilon\to0$时一致成立，且对速度场的$C^k$扰动保持稳定，最终得到光滑快速发电机向量场的开集，由此封闭了Fast Dynamo猜想。

Conclusion: 该工作从数学上严格证实了快速发电机现象可以在光滑、自治（非时间周期）的速度场中实现，并具有结构稳定性（$C^k$-开性），为理解天体物理与地球物理中磁场的自激发机制提供了严格的理论基础。

Abstract: We construct a nonempty $C^k$-open family, for some $k\in\mathbb{N}$, of smooth, autonomous, divergence-free velocity fields on $\mathbb{T}^3$ that generate fast dynamos. This resolves the Fast Dynamo Conjecture of Zeldovich and Sakharov, as recorded in Arnold's book of problems. We first construct a family of smooth, time-periodic fast dynamos via a stretch-fold-shear mechanism that applies to a large class of velocity fields. We then realize the associated period dynamics within a smooth autonomous flow using a global Poincaré section and a suitably prescribed return-time function. In both settings, the proof first isolates unstable distributional eigenmodes for the ideal induction operator on an anisotropic Banach space of distributions and then shows that this spectral instability persists under the singular perturbation $\varepsilon Δ$. The resulting estimates are uniform as $\varepsilon\to0$ and stable under $C^k$ perturbations of the velocity field, yielding an open set of smooth fast dynamo vector fields.

</details>

*Xu Zhuang*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明了Peter Beelen等人提出的一个猜想：对于由y^m=x^i(x^2+1)定义的F_{q^2}-极大函数域F_i，其几何自同构群的阶恰好为4(q+1)。通过建立反向不等式证明了该等式的成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前研究已证明该函数域的几何自同构群包含一个4(q+1)阶的子群，但尚不确定是否还存在更大的自同构群。作者希望证实该猜想，从而完全确定该极大函数域的几何自同构群结构。

Method: 通过建立反向不等式（即证明几何自同构群的阶不超过4(q+1)），从而与已知的下界相结合，得出恰好等于4(q+1)的结论。

Result: 成功证明了几何自同构群的阶恰好等于4(q+1)，验证了Beelen等人提出的猜想。

Conclusion: 该函数域F_i的完整几何自同构群已被确定，其阶为4(q+1)，解决了此前的猜想。

Abstract: Let $q=p^h>7$ be odd, put $m=(q+1)/2$, and suppose that $i=(m-2)/2$ satisfies $\gcd(i,m)=\gcd(i+2,m)=1$. For the $\mathbf{F}_{q^2}$-maximal function field $\mathcal{F}_i=\mathbf{F}_{q^2}(x,y)$ defined by $y^m=x^i(x^2+1)$, Peter Beelen, Maria Montanucci, Jonathan Niemann, and Luciane Quoos showed that the geometric automorphism group contains a subgroup of order $4(q+1)$ and conjectured that its order is exactly $4(q+1)$. We prove this equality by establishing the reverse inequality.

</details>

Abstract: In this article we focus on the solutions of a congruence equation: $\binom{2n}{n}\equiv 2^n \bmod n$. Using the main result of this article and the SageMath software, we improve largely the number of known solutions. Furthermore, we prove that some famous numbers like even perfect numbers and Wieferich primes are connected to solutions of this equation.

Method: 作者通过将Sutherland哈密顿量的谱问题转化为非齐次二次形式的研究，利用Marklof定理的推广版本（包括有限同余和Weyl扇区版本），从而证明了谱的泊松对相关性质。此方法将A₂型根系的代数结构与丢番图逼近的算术性质相结合。

Result: 成功证明了三粒子排斥三角Sutherland哈密顿量满足Berry-Tabor定理，即其去对称化谱呈现泊松对相关统计。

Conclusion: 该结果为正定A₂型相关可积系统的谱统计提供了严格的数学证明，进一步丰富了Berry-Tabor猜想在具体多体可积系统上的验证。

Abstract: We prove a Berry-Tabor theorem for the repulsive three-particle trigonometric Sutherland Hamiltonian. For fixed total momentum, its relative spectrum is an affine translate of the positive-definite $A_2$-form. For a Diophantine coupling, the desymmetrized spectrum has Poissonian pair correlation. The proof reduces the problem to a finite-congruence and Weyl-sector version of Marklof's theorem on inhomogeneous quadratic forms.

</details>


### [98] [Skolem-Mahler-Lech in rings of positive characteristic: a shorter proof and a multi-dimensional generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03127)
*Ruiwen Dong,Doron Shafrir*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文给出Dong-Shafrir关于交换环上线性指数映射零点集结论的一个更短证明（以Derksen-Masser定理为黑箱），并证明有限特征环上n维线性递归序列零点集的Skolem-Mahler-Lech定理多维推广，即零点集可分解为有效可计算的p-正规集之交。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Dong和Shafrir（2026）推广了Derksen-Masser（2012）关于正特征域上S-单位方程解集的定理，将其延伸至交换环上满足M=0的线性指数映射，证明了零点集是有效可计算的p-正规集的交集。本文旨在两方面推进：一是对该结果给出更短的替代证明，并揭示零点集的结构（正布尔组合与仿射变换）以获得更深层次的理解；二是利用该方法将Skolem-Mahler-Lech定理推广到有限特征环上n维线性递归序列的情形，从而为经典线性递归序列在特征为p^a或p^a q^b环上的公共值判定问题提供决策程序。

Method: 1) 对Dong-Shafrir定理给出新证明：将Derksen-Masser定理作为黑箱使用，避免重复复杂的构造；新证明将零点集分解为正布尔组合（positive Boolean combination）的形式，即零点集可表示为零集（域上线性指数方程零点集）的仿射变换的有限并/交组合。2) 将上述方法应用于n维线性递归序列：证明当M满足M=0（对素数）时，零点集是交集。核心工具是p-正规集（p-normal sets）和仿射变换分解技术。

Result: 1) 获得了Dong-Shafrir定理的更短证明，且零点集被分解为域上线性指数方程零点集的仿射变换的正布尔组合。2) 证明了n维Skolem-Mahler-Lech定理在有限特征环上的推广：线性递归序列的零点集（在N^n中）是有效可计算的p-正规集之交。作为推论，得到了特征为p^a或p^a q^b的环上两条经典线性递归序列是否存在公共值的决策算法。

Conclusion: 本文通过简化Dong-Shafrir定理的证明（以Derksen-Masser为黑箱并给出零点集的布尔组合结构），以及对Skolem-Mahler-Lech定理的n维有限特征推广，为该领域提供了更清晰的证明路径和更强的可判定性结果，特别是对特征为素数幂或两个素数之幂乘积的环，给出了线性递归序列公共值的决策程序。

Abstract: Let $R$ be a commutative ring and $f(a_1, \ldots, a_n) = \sum_{i=1}^k r_{i1}^{a_1} \cdots r_{in}^{a_n} m_i$ be a linear-exponential map over an $R$-module $M$. Dong and Shafrir (2026) showed that, when $\ell M = 0$ for some $\ell \in \mathbb{N}_{>0}$, the zero set of $f$ is the intersection of effectively computable $p$-normal sets, where $p$ ranges over the prime divisors of $\ell$. This generalizes an earlier theorem of Derksen and Masser (2012) on the solution set of $S$-unit equations over fields of positive characteristic. The purpose of this paper is twofold. First, we give a shorter proof of Dong and Shafrir's result, using the theorem of Derksen-Masser as a blackbox. Our proof also yields a decomposition of the zero set as a positive Boolean combination of affine transformations of zero sets of linear-exponential equations over fields. Second, we prove a multi-dimensional generalization of the Skolem-Mahler-Lech theorem over rings of finite characteristic. Specifically, we show that the zero set of every $n$-dimensional linear recurrence sequence over an $R$-module $M$ satisfying $\ell M = 0$ is the intersection of effectively computable $p$-normal sets (in $\mathbb{N}^n$), where $p$ ranges over the prime divisors of $\ell$. For example, this gives a decision procedure for whether two classical linear recurrence sequences have a common value over a ring of characteristic $p^a$ or $p^a q^b$, where $p$ and $q$ are primes.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受Schoof-Pila型点计数算法的启发，需要理解有限域上亏格3超椭圆曲线Jacobian上ℓ-挠点的Galois轨道结构，以改进点计数效率。

Method: 通过细致分析Frobenius在Jacobian J上的Jordan分解及其幂零部分，研究ℓ-挠点的定义域次数和因子分解模式，以建立ℓ-挠子群秩与Galois轨道之间的对应关系。
